21:T3206f,<h2 id="들어가기-전에-vs-cypress">들어가기 전에.. (vs. Cypress)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#들어가기-전에-vs-cypress"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="cypress에서-playwright로-왜-넘어갔나">Cypress에서 Playwright로 왜 넘어갔나??<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#cypress에서-playwright로-왜-넘어갔나"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<h4 id="cypress-개발-시-어려움-점">Cypress 개발 시 어려움 점<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#cypress-개발-시-어려움-점"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<ul>
<li>테스트 환경 로딩, 테스트 수행 시 너무 많은 시간이 소요한다는 느낌을 받았습니다.</li>
<li>테스트 환경에서 엘리먼트를 Cypress 선택 시 못 찾는 경우가 다수 발생했고, 원인을 찾을 수 없었습니다.</li>
<li>Cypress 만에 문법이 FE 개발자분들에게 익숙하지 않았습니다.</li>
<li>테스트 속도 개선을 위해서 병렬 테스트 하고 싶어도 추가적인 금액을 지불해야하는 어려움이 있었습니다.</li>
</ul>
<p>→ 해당 어려움을 극복하기 위해서 다른 테스트 라이브러리를 찾아보게 되었고, 찾던 중에 위에 어려움점을 해결해 줄 수 있는 라이브러리를 하나를 발견하게 되었습니다. 바로, 마이크로소프트에서 만든 Playwright 입니다.</p>
<h4 id="cypress-vs-playwright">Cypress vs Playwright<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#cypress-vs-playwright"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<ul>
<li>테스트 속도 비교
<ul>
<li>e2e 테스트 고립을 위해서 링크 이동부터 진행 할 경우 spec 1개 기준으로는 볼 경우 Cypress 보다는 엄청 빠르진 않았다.
<ul>
<li>단, 병렬로 실행할 경우 case 가 많아 질 경우 테스트 속도가 빨라지는 걸 느낄 수 있었다.</li>
<li><a href="https://emewjin.github.io/playwright-vs-cypress/#%ED%85%8C%EC%8A%A4%ED%8A%B8-%EC%86%8D%EB%8F%84%EA%B0%80-cypress%EB%B3%B4%EB%8B%A4-%EC%A7%84%EC%A7%9C-%EB%8D%94-%EB%B9%A0%EB%A5%BC%EA%B9%8C">https://emewjin.github.io/playwright-vs-cypress/#테스트-속도가-cypress보다-진짜-더-빠를까</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Playwright beforeAll 함수를 제공하고 있어서 SPA 구조로 되어 있는 웹에서는 초기에 링크 이동 후 page.goBack() 이동 시 beforeEach 대비해서 테스트 속도가 빨라지는 걸 느낄 수 있었다.(단, beforeAll에서는 page 얻어올 수 없어서 새로 만들어야하는데 그럴 경우 병렬 실행을 할 수가 없었다)</li>
<li>회사에 프로젝트에 추가해 본 결과 대략 5배 정도 빨리진다는 걸 알 수 있었습니다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>테스트 지원 환경
<ul>
<li>Playwright
<ul>
<li>크로미움 기반 브라우저, 파이어폭스, <strong>사파리 지원</strong></li>
<li><strong>모바일 브라우저 제공</strong></li>
<li><strong>실험모드: 안드로이드 크롬/웹뷰, Electron 지원예정</strong></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Cypress
<ul>
<li>크로미움 기반의 브라우저, 파이어폭스 2가지만 지원</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>병렬 테스트 지원
<ul>
<li>Playwright
<ul>
<li><strong>모든 테스트를 병렬로 진행</strong></li>
<li><strong>최대 4개까지 worker thread 세팅 가능</strong>
<ul>
<li>단 worker thread 지정 시 테스트 단위로 고립해서 작성이 필수!!</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Cypress
<ul>
<li>모든 테스트를 직렬로 진행</li>
<li>단, 병렬 테스트 진행 시 유료 플랜을 가입해야지 CI/CD 환경에서 별렬 테스트를 수행 가능 → 돈을 내야해요ㅜㅜ)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>많은 이벤트 지원
<ul>
<li>hover, drag 지원(cypress 미지원)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>문법
<ul>
<li>지원하는 언어에 대한 순수 문법을 지원한다.(cypress에서는 자체 문법을 제공)
<img src="/blog-assets/2023/04/assets/02/Untitled.png" alt="Untitled"></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="playwright-소개">Playwright 소개<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-소개"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p><img src="/blog-assets/2023/04/assets/02/Untitled_1.png" alt="Untitled"></p>
<p>Playwright는 MS에서 만든 오픈소스 웹 테스트, 자동화 라이브러리 입니다. 하나의 API로 Chromium, Firefox, WebKit, 모웹(Chromium, Safari등)까지 테스트 할 수 있습니다.(<a href="https://playwright.dev/docs/next/api/class-android">Next 버전</a>에서는 ADB를 이용한 Android Chrome, Electron )도 지원하는 걸로 보입니다)</p>
<p>사실, Playwright 자체는 E2E 테스트 프레임워크가 아니고, @playwright/test 프레임워크를 사용하여서 크로스 브라우징 테스트가 가능합니다. (단, 레거시 Edge나 IE 지원하지 않음)</p>
<h3 id="playwright-특징">playwright 특징<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-특징"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>특징
하나의 API로 멀티 브라우저/플랫폼 테스트 가능
<ul>
<li>Cross-browser: 최신 모던 브라우저인 Chromium, Webkit, Firefox 지원</li>
<li>Cross-platform: Windows, Linux, MacOS, locally or CI, headless or headed</li>
<li>Cross-language: Typescript, Javascript, Python, .NET, Java</li>
<li>Test Mobile Web: 네이티브 모바일 에뮬레이션을 활용해서 모웹 안드로이드/사파리 테스트
그외 기능</li>
<li>자동 대기: 액션이 실행되기 전까지 엘리먼트가 준비될때까지 자동 기다림(timeout 옵션)</li>
<li>재시도: 필요한 조건이 충족될때까지 검사가 자동 재시도(retry 옵션)</li>
<li>Tracing: 실행 추적, 비디오, 스키린샷 캡쳐를 통해서 오류 추적 가능</li>
<li>네트워크 요청을 모킹하기 위한 네트워크 활동 훅킹 가능</li>
<li>마우스, 키보드의 기본 입력 이벤트 테스트 지원
강력한 툴</li>
<li>Codegen: Playwright 기능을 바탕으로 자동화 테스트 코드를 생성해주는 도구
<ul>
<li>자동으로 코드를 생성해주기 때문에, 코드 작성 시간과 노력을 절약 가능</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>inspector: 테스트 실행 단계별 디버깅 실행(크롬 개발자 도구 축소버전)</li>
<li>trace viewer: 테스트 실패를 조사하기 위해 모든 정보를 캡쳐</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="playwright-구축">Playwright 구축<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-구축"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="test-파일">Test 파일<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#test-파일"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>파일명에 test를 포함해야합니다. hotfix.test.ts 와 같은 형태의 파일명을 가집니다.</p>
<ul>
<li>playwright.config.ts에서 testMatch를 통해서 확장자 변경은 가능합니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="timeouts">Timeouts<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#timeouts"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>playwright는 여러 태스크 별로 별도에 타임아웃을 가지고 있습니다.</li>
<li>글로벌/커맨드 별로 타임아웃 설정이 가능합니다.
<ul>
<li>간소화 테스트 전역 타임아웃: 2초(timeout: 60 _ 1000 _ 2)</li>
<li>커맨드 타임아웃 설정
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'[class*="modal"]'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'text=취소하기'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ timeout: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">40000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span></code></pre></figure>













































<table><thead><tr><th>Timeout</th><th>Default</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Test timeout</td><td>30000 ms</td><td>Timeout for each test, includes test, hooks and fixtures:SET DEFAULTconfig = { timeout: 60000 }OVERRIDEtest.setTimeout(120000)</td></tr><tr><td>Expect timeout</td><td>5000 ms</td><td>Timeout for each assertion:SET DEFAULTconfig = { expect: { timeout: 10000 } }OVERRIDEexpect(locator).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 })</td></tr><tr><td>Action timeout</td><td>no timeout</td><td>Timeout for each action:SET DEFAULTconfig = { use: { actionTimeout: 10000 } }OVERRIDElocator.click({ timeout: 10000 })</td></tr><tr><td>Navigation timeout</td><td>no timeout</td><td>Timeout for each navigation action:SET DEFAULTconfig = { use: { navigationTimeout: 30000 } }OVERRIDEpage.goto('/', { timeout: 30000 })</td></tr><tr><td>Global timeout</td><td>no timeout</td><td>Global timeout for the whole test run:SET IN CONFIGconfig = { globalTimeout: 60<em>60</em>1000 }</td></tr><tr><td>beforeAll/afterAll timeout</td><td>30000 ms</td><td>Timeout for the hook:SET IN HOOKtest.setTimeout(60000)</td></tr><tr><td>Fixture timeout</td><td>no timeout</td><td>Timeout for an individual fixture:SET IN FIXTURE{ scope: 'test', timeout: 30000 }</td></tr></tbody></table>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>팁
<ul>
<li>테스트케이스는 읽기 쉽고 의미 전달이 명확한 한글로 작성하는 것이 좋을 것 같습니다.</li>
<li>카카오나 NHN, 클래스101 등에서도 한글로 테스트케이스를 작성하는 것으로 보입니다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="cli">CLI<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#cli"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>다음의 명령어로 터미널에서 테스트를 수행할 수 있습니다. 기본적으로 <strong>headless</strong> 로 동작합니다.
headless 란?
GUI환경이 아닌 브라우저를 뜻합니다. CLI (Command Line Interface)에서 동작하는 것을 의미합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">yarn playwright test</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>run 옵션 참고: <a href="https://playwright.dev/docs/test-cli">https://playwright.dev/docs/test-cli</a>
환경설정 파일에 설정된 브라우저/플랫폼에 대해서 테스트가 수행됩니다.
playwright에 내장된 에뮬레이터에서 제공되는 브라우저/플랫폼(chromium, firefox, safari)를 지정하여 테스트를 수행할 수 있습니다.
<a href="https://playwright.dev/docs/emulation">https://playwright.dev/docs/emulation</a>
테스트 고려 사항</p>
<h3 id="테스트-속도-개선">테스트 속도 개선<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#테스트-속도-개선"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>테스트 수행 중에 가장 시간이 많이 소요되는 부분에 대해서는 테스트 진행 전에 수행할 수 있도록 처리해야합니다.</li>
<li>속도가 늦어 질 경우 배포 이후에 빠른 대응을 못함으로 꼭 테스트 수행 시간에 대해서 고민 후 개발을 진행해주시면 좋을 것 같습니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// context, page 생성 시간 단축을 위해서 테스트 전에 한번만 수행</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">beforeAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">browser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">baseURL</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">contextOptions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> context</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browser.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(contextOptions);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  page </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> context.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newPage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goto</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">beforeEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">isMobile</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (isMobile) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'[aria-label="navigation-button"]'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'[aria-label="about-link"]:visible'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">        '[aria-label="desktop-navigation"] [aria-label="about-link"]:visible'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="-테스트-전략-">⭐ 테스트 전략 ⭐<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#-테스트-전략-"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="병렬-테스트">병렬 테스트<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#병렬-테스트"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>각 테스트 별로 의존적이지 않도록 테스트 코드를 고립시키는 가이드 제공
<ul>
<li>현재는 단일 스레드로만 동작하도록 세팅</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="멀티-플랫폼브라우저-테스트">멀티 플랫폼/브라우저 테스트<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#멀티-플랫폼브라우저-테스트"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>서비스만에 타겟 멀티 플랫폼/브라우저 전략을 세워야 할 것 같습니다.</li>
<li>다른 회사 사례
<ul>
<li>하이퍼커넥트: safari를 필수로 테스트 진행
<ul>
<li><a href="https://hyperconnect.github.io/2022/01/28/e2e-test-with-playwright.html">https://hyperconnect.github.io/2022/01/28/e2e-test-with-playwright.html</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="playwright-작성법">playwright 작성법<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-작성법"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="테스트케이스-작성법">테스트케이스 작성법<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#테스트케이스-작성법"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p><strong>Run Command Line</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Running all tests
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> playwright</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> test</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>Running a single test file
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test landing</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">page.spec.ts</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>Run a set of test files
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test tests</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">todo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> tests</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">landing</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>Run files that have <code>landing</code> or <code>login</code> in the file name
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test landing login</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>Run the test with the title
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">g </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"add a todo item"</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>Running tests in headed mode
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test landing</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">page.spec.ts </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">--</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">headed</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>Running tests on a specific project
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test landing</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">page.ts </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">--</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">project</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">chromium</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<strong>Debugging Tests</strong></li>
<li>Debugging all tests:
<pre><code>npx playwright test --debug
</code></pre>
</li>
<li>Debugging one test file:
<pre><code>npx playwright test example.spec.ts --debug
</code></pre>
</li>
<li>Debugging a test from the line number where the <code>test(..</code> is defined:
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">npx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> playwright</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> example.spec.ts:10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> --debug</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<strong>기본적인 테스트케이스 구성</strong>
<strong>Project Dependencies</strong></li>
<li>테스트 코드 수행 전에 꼭 필요한 테스트를 진행해야 할 경우 global setup action을 작성해주세요.</li>
<li>Desktop Chrome 진행 전 dependencies에 연관된 setup 파일들을 미리 수행되게 됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// playwright.config.ts</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'setup'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">testMatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#72F088;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">\.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">setup</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#72F088;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">\.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">ts/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'chromium'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">devices[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Desktop Chrome'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  dependencies</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'setup'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">},</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>Configure globalSetup and globalTeardown</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>모든 테스트 전에 한번에 수행되야하는 코드에 대해서는 globalSetup/globalTeardown 속성을 적용해주세요.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// playwright.config.ts/js</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { defineConfig } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@playwright/test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> defineConfig</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  globalSetup: require.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'./global-setup'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  globalTeardown: require.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'./global-teardown'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  use: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    baseURL: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'http://localhost:3000/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    storageState: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'state.json'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">})</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>describe, test</strong>
describe로 테스트들을 논리적으로 나누고, test로 테스트를 작성합니다.
describe 내에서 테스트 전체 선행/후행 작업 필요 시 beforeAll, AfterEach를 사용해보고,
<strong>→ context, page 생성 시 시간이 많이 소요됨으로 전체 테스트 전에 미리 생성해두면 테스트 시간을 줄일 수 있습니다.</strong>
각 테스트 별로 선행/후행 작업 필요 시 beforeEach, afterEach를 사용해주세요.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { expect, Page, test, BrowserContext } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@playwright/test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">describe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'홈'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browserContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> BrowserContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // beforeAll hook은 최초 딱 한번 실행. initialize 작업 등을 수행</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">beforeAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">browser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">contextOptions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    browserContext </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browser.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(contextOptions);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">beforeEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">isMobile</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // 페이지 생성</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    page </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browserContext.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newPage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goto</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (isMobile) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'[aria-label="navigation-button"]'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'[aria-label="career-link"]:visible'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">          '[aria-label="desktop-navigation"] [aria-label="career-link"]:visible'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">afterEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">close</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">afterAll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browserContext.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">close</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'02-5. Carrer 상세 화면에 대한 검증을 한다.'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'main > section > ul > li a'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> element.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getAttribute</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'href'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    element.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveURL</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(href);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> subElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'main section > div'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(subElement).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeVisible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>테스트 제목</strong>
테스트 제목은 최대한 명시적으로 제목만 보아도 어떤 테스트를 수행하는 지 알 수 있어야하며, 능동형 문장으로 작성합니다. (~~된다 라는 표현은 지양하고 ~~한다 라는 표현으로 작성해요)</p>
<h3 id="사소한-꿀팁">사소한 꿀팁<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#사소한-꿀팁"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<h4 id="testonly">test.only<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#testonly"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>테스트 파일안에 여러 테스트 케이스들이 있는데 하나의 테스트만 수정해도 파일 내의 모든 테스트 케이스들이 실행됩니다. 작성 중인 테스트 케이스만 실행되도록 <strong>test.only</strong> 를 사용할 수 있습니다.</p>
<h4 id="testskip">test.skip<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#testskip"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<p>실행 불가능한 테스트 케이스지만 테스트 목록에는 넣고 싶을 때 <strong>test.skip</strong>을 사용할 수 있습니다. 최종 결과에 pending으로 집계됩니다.
<strong>Selector</strong>
playwright에 selector는 쿼리 우선순위를 기반으로 제공되며, 해당 기능이 안될 locator를 이용해서 자유롭게 엘리먼트에 접근이 가능합니다.
쿼리 우선 순위</p>
<ul>
<li>시각 요소/ 사용자의 인터렉션을 테스트하기 위한 쿼리들 간 우선순위
<ul>
<li>getByRole > getByLabelText > getByPlaceholderText > getByText > getByDisplayValue</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>HTML5 와 ARIA 속성을 테스트 하기 위한 쿼리들 간 우선순위
<ul>
<li>getByAltText > getByTitle</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>테스트 ID: 위에 쿼리들로도 불가능한 값을 검색하기 위해서만 사용
<ul>
<li>getByTestId</li>
<li>data attribute는 팀 내 합의로 정하는 것이 좋을 것 같습니다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>해당 우선순위로도 접근이 불가능할 경우 locator 사용을 해주시면 됩니다.
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'main > section > ul > li a'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<strong>테스트 도구 활용</strong>
Playwright Inspector 화면에서 Pick locator 클릭 후 화면을 선택 시 자동으로 테스트 코드를 생성해줍니다.
해당 기능을 통해서 코드 생성 시 참고가 가능하면, 개발 생산성을 높일 수 있을 것 같습니다.</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="negating-matchers">Negating Matchers<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#negating-matchers"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>일반적으로 matcher 앞에 .not을 붙여주면 됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(value).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(locator).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toContainText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'some text'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="soft-assertions">Soft Assertions<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#soft-assertions"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>기본적으로 Assertion이 에러가 날 경우 테스트가 종료되지만, Soft Assertions 적용 시 테스트가 종료되지 않고 실패에 대해서 마킹만 해주게 됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Make a few checks that will not stop the test when failed...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'status'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Success'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'eta'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'1 day'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ... and continue the test to check more things.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'link'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'next page'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'heading'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Make another order'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeVisible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="polling">Polling<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#polling"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>expect.poll사용하여서 비동기적으로 기다릴 때 사용하게 됩니다.(API 응답을 할 경우 대부분 사용)</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">poll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://api.example.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // Custom error message, optional.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      message: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'make sure API eventually succeeds'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// custom error message</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // Poll for 10 seconds; defaults to 5 seconds. Pass 0 to disable timeout.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      timeout: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">poll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://api.example.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // Probe, wait 1s, probe, wait 2s, probe, wait 10s, probe, wait 10s, probe, .... Defaults to [100, 250, 500, 1000].</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      intervals: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      timeout: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">60_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="retrying">Retrying<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#retrying"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>코드가 블락 될 경우 intervals를 통해서 재시도 처리를 해주게 됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://api.example.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toPass</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Probe, wait 1s, probe, wait 2s, probe, wait 10s, probe, wait 10s, probe, .... Defaults to [100, 250, 500, 1000].</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  intervals: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  timeout: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">60_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="playwright-가이드">Playwright 가이드<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-가이드"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>가이드
Playwright 기본</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Install playwright</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"># 브라우저 설치</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">$</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> -D</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> playwright</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"># 테스팅 라이브러리 설치</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">$</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> npm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> -D</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> @playwright/test</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="playwright-주요개념">Playwright 주요개념<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-주요개념"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">chromium</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> require</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'playwright'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> browser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> chromium.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">launch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browser.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">close</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>브라우저 인스턴스를 만드는 데 비용이 많이 들기 때문에 Playwright는 단일 인스턴스가 여러 브라우저 컨텍스트를 통해 수행할 수 있는 작업을 극대화하도록 설계되었다.
브라우저 컨텍스트는 브라우저 인스턴스 내에서 분리된 유사 세션이다. 만드는데 빠르고 비용이 적다. 각 테스트를 새로운 브라우저 컨텍스트에서 실행하여 브라우저 상태를 테스트 간에 분리하는 것이 좋다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> browser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> chromium.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">launch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> context</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browser.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>컨텍스트별로 다른 기기를 시뮬레이션 할 수 있다. 예제에서는 아이폰과 아이패드에 대한 컨텍스트를 생성하고 있다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { devices } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'playwright'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> iPhone</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> devices[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'iPhone 11 Pro'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> iPad</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> devices[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'iPad Pro 11'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> iPhoneContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browser.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">iPhone,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  permissions: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'geolocation'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  geolocation: { latitude: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">52.52</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, longitude: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">13.39</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  colorScheme: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'dark'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  locale: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'de-DE'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> iPadContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> browser.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">iPad,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  permissions: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'geolocation'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  geolocation: { latitude: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">37.4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, longitude: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">127.1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>컨텍스트는 컨텍스트 내의 단일 탭 또는 팝업 창을 나타내는 페이지를 가질 수 있다. URL로 이동해서 페이지의 콘텐츠와 상호작용할 때 사용하면 된다.
페이지를 이용해서 각기 다른 탭에서 작업하는 것처럼 할 수 있다. 예제에서는 각기 다른 페이지를 탐색한다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 페이지1 생성</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> page1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> context.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newPage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 브라우저에 URL을 입력하는 것처럼 탐색</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page1.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goto</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'http://example.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 인풋 채우기</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page1.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'#search'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'query'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 페이지2 생성</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> page2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> context.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">newPage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page2.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goto</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://www.nhn.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 링크를 클릭하여 탐색</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'.nav_locale'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 새로운 url 출력</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">url</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>페이지는 다시 1개 이상의 프레임 객체를 가질 수 있다. 각 페이지에는 메인 프레임이 있고 페이지 레벨 상호작용(클릭 등)이 메인 프레임에서 작동하는 것으로 가정한다. iframe 태그와 함께 추가 프레임을 가질 수 있다.
프레임을 이용해서 프레임 내부에 있는 엘리먼트를 가져오거나 조작할 수 있다. 예제에서는 프레임을 가져오는 방법들과 프레임 내부의 엘리먼트와 상호작용하는 방법을 보여준다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 프레임의 name 속성으로 프레임 가져오기</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> frame</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">frame</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'frame-login'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 프레임의 URL로 가져오기</span></span>
