<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> frame</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">frame</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ url:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">domain</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 선택자로 프레임 가져오기</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> frameElementHandle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">$</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'.frame-class'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> frame</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> frameElementHandle.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">contentFrame</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 프레임과 상호작용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> frame.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'#username-input'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'John'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="playwright-commands">Playwright Commands<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-commands"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<h4 id="actions">Actions<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#actions"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<ul>
<li>
<p>input, textarea, [contenteditable] 엘리먼트를 포커스 &#x26; 텍스트를 바로 입력할 경우 호출
locator.fill() 대체해서 사용 가능</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Text input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'textbox'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Peter'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Date input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Birth date'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'2020-02-02'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Time input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Appointment time'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'13:15'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Local datetime input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Local time'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'2020-03-02T05:15'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>Type characters → real keyboard type
입력 엘리먼트에 실제 키보드 입력이 되는것처럼 동작
locator.type() 대체해서 사용 가능</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Type character by character</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'#area'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hello World!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>엘리먼트를 선택하거나 단축키를 생성하기 위한 동작
keyboardEvent.key 이벤트를 사용이 가능
<a href="http://locator.press/">locator.press</a>() 대체 가능</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Hit Enter</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Submit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">press</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Enter'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'textbox'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">press</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'$'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

</li>
<li>
<p>input[type=checkbox], input[type=radio] and [role=checkbox] 엘리먼트에 대해서 체크/미체크 세팅, 얻어올 경우 호출
locator.setChecked() 대체해서 사용 가능</p>

</li>
<li>
<p>select 엘리먼트를 단일/다중 선택 시 사용
특정 옵션에 value, label 선택이 가능
locator.selectOption() 대체해서 사용가능</p>

</li>
<li>
<p>DOM이 접근이 가능할 때까지 대기</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>DOM이 보여질 때까지 대기(no empty, no display:none, no visibility:hidden)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>움직임이 멈출때까지 대기(예: transition finishes)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>뷰포트 안에 엘리먼트가 보여질 경우(scroll)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>엘리먼트가 다른 엘리먼트에 가려지지 않을 때까지 대기</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>엘리먼트가 detach 된다면 다시 attach 될때까지 대기</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>강제클릭: 다른 엘리먼트에 의해서 가려질 경우 강제로 클릭 가능</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>dispatchEvent: 사용자의 입력과 상관없이 클릭 이벤트만 전달하고 싶은경우</p>
</li>
</ul>

<ul>
<li>
<p>input 엘리먼트 type=”file”인 경우 사용이 가능
locator.setInputFiles() 대체 가능
엘리먼트가 동적으로 생성된 경우 waitForEvent로 대기 가능(page.on(’filechooser’) 대체 가능)</p>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Upload file'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setInputFiles</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'myfile.pdf'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Select multiple files</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Upload files'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setInputFiles</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'file1.txt'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'file2.txt'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Remove all the selected files</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Upload file'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setInputFiles</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Upload buffer from memory</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Upload file'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setInputFiles</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'file.txt'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  mimeType: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'text/plain'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  buffer: Buffer.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'this is test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Start waiting for file chooser before clicking. Note no await.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fileChooserPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">waitForEvent</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'filechooser'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Upload file'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fileChooser.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setFiles</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'myfile.pdf'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>locator.focus() 함수로 대체 가능</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Password'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">focus</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>locator.dragTo() 대체 가능
lower-level 함수로 대체할 경우(locator.hover, mouse.down, mouse.move, mouse.up 사용가능)</p>
</li>
</ul>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// lower-level</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'#item-to-be-dragged'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">hover</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>

<h3 id="assertions">Assertions<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#assertions"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p><a href="https://playwright.dev/docs/test-assertions">https://playwright.dev/docs/test-assertions</a>
Assertion은 기대값과 결과값을 검사하기 위해 사용합니다.
Playwright test assertion 수행 시 expect 함수를 사용할 수 있으며, 다양한 assertions 함수를 제공하고 있습니다.
<br>
<br></p>

<ul>
<li>일반적으로 matcher 앞에 .not을 붙여주면 됩니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(value).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toEqual</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(locator).not.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toContainText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'some text'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>기본적으로 테스트 실패 시 실행이 종료되지만, soft assertion 일 경우에는 종료가 되지 않는다.
실패 여부를 체크하기 위해서 <a href="http://test.info/">test.info</a> 사용 가능</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Make a few checks that will not stop the test when failed...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'status'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Success'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ... and continue the test to check more things.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'link'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'next page'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'heading'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Make another order'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeVisible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// test.info</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//// Make a few checks that will not stop the test when failed...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'status'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Success'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//// Avoid running further if there were soft assertion failures.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">info</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().errors).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveLength</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>특정 에러 메시지를 표현
expect.soft 대체 가능</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Name'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'should be logged in'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeVisible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: should be logged </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">in</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Call log:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   - expect.toBeVisible with timeout 5000ms</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   - waiting for "getByText('Name')"</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   2 |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   3 | test('example test', async({ page }) => {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// > 4 |   await expect(page.getByText('Name'), 'should be logged in').toBeVisible();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//     |                                                                  ^</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   5 | });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//   6 |</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 동일한 코드</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(value, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'my soft assertion'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">56</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>expect.poll사용하여서 비동기적으로 기다릴 때 사용하게 됩니다.(API 응답을 할 경우 대부분 사용)</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">poll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // Custom error message, optional.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      message: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'make sure API eventually succeeds'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// custom error message</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // Poll for 10 seconds; defaults to 5 seconds. Pass 0 to disable timeout.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      timeout: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">poll</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'&#x3C;https://api.example.com>'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // Probe, wait 1s, probe, wait 2s, probe, wait 10s, probe, wait 10s, probe, .... Defaults to [100, 250, 500, 1000].</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      intervals: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      timeout: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">60_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  )</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>코드가 블락 될 경우 intervals를 통해서 재시도 처리를 해주게 됩니다</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// API 응답이 성공할 때까지 코드 블록을 재 실행</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'&#x3C;https://api.example.com>'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toPass</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// API 응답에 재시도 간격에 대해서 사용자 지정 시간을 세팅</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'&#x3C;https://api.example.com>'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(response.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBe</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toPass</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Probe, wait 1s, probe, wait 2s, probe, wait 10s, probe, wait 10s, probe, .... Defaults to [100, 250, 500, 1000].</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  intervals: [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">10_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  timeout: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">60_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="locators">Locators<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#locators"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>page.getByRole() → 접근성 속성을 찾을 경우</p>
<ul>
<li>보조 기술이 페이지를 인식하는 방법을 사용할 경우 사용
page.getByLabel() → form 컨트롤 라벨 텍스트 찾을 경우</li>
<li>form fields 찾을 경우 사용
page.getByPlaceholder() → placeholder 찾은 경우</li>
<li>엘리먼트에 label이 없고 placeholder 텍스트가 있는경우 사용
page.getByText() → 텍스트 찾을 경우</li>
<li>인터렉션이 없는 엘리먼트의 텍스트를 찾을 경우(div, span, p) 사용</li>
<li>인터렉션이 있는 엘리먼트(button, a, input): page.getByLabel
page.getByAltText() → 대체 텍스트를 찾을 경우</li>
<li>img, area 엘리먼트 처럼 대체 텍스트가 있는 경우 사용
page.getByTitle() → 타이틀 속성을 찾을 경우</li>
<li>엘리먼트 title 속성이 있는경우
page.getByTestId() → data-testid 속성을 찾을 경우</li>
<li>text, role 사용할 없거나, testid를 사용이 가능할 경우 사용</li>
<li>엘리먼트 내부에 특정 값을 검색할 경우 filter 사용</li>
<li>hasText: 텍스트 검색, has: locator 검색</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// hasText</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Add to cart'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// has</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'listitem'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ has: page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'heading'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Product 2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Add to cart'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>특정 엘리먼트 배열의 인덱스 접근 가능</li>

</ul>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  list.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> element.textContent),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="auto-waiting">Auto-waiting<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#auto-waiting"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>모든 관련 검사가 통과할 때까지 자동 대기한 이후에 다음 요청된 작업을 수행합니다.
예를 들어서, page.click()의 경우 playwright는 다음을 확인합니다</p>
<ul>
<li>엘리먼트가 DOM에 연결된 경우</li>
<li>엘리먼트가 보여질 경우</li>
<li>엘리먼트가 애니메이션 완료, 동작안하는 경우</li>
<li>엘리먼트가 다른 요소에 가려지지 않는 경우</li>
<li>엘리먼트가 활성화 된 경우











































































































































































































<table><thead><tr><th>Action</th><th><a href="https://playwright.dev/docs/actionability#attached">Attached</a></th><th><a </li>
</ul>
<h2 id="best-practices">Best Practices<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#best-practices"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="testing-philosophy">Testing philosophy<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#testing-philosophy"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>사용자에게 보이는 영역/행위 테스트</p>
<ul>
<li>최종 사용자가 페이지를 보거나, 상호작용이 가능한 페이지에 대해서 테스트를 수행한다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>테스트 작성 시 가능하면 고립</p>
<ul>
<li>테스트 각각에 대해서 격리해서 작업(local/session storage, data, cookies등)</li>
<li>테스트에 특정 반복을 피해야할 경우 before and after hooks를 이용</li>
<li>테스트 파일 내에서 특정 URL 이동, 앱의 이부에 로그인 진행 시 before hooks 이용</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { test } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '@playwright/test'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">test.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">beforeEach</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Runs before each test and signs in each page.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goto</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://github.com/login'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Username or email address'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'username'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Password'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'password'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Sign in'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'first'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // page is signed in.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'second'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // page is signed in.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>전체적인 흐름이 맞는 테스트 코드 작성(긴 테스트도 괜찮다.)</p>
<ul>
<li>전체 앱 흐름을 테스트 할 경우 여러 작업에 대한 assertion 처리로 괜찮다.</li>
<li>긴 테스트를 굳이 개별 테스트로 나눌 경우 테스트 실행 속도를 늦추기 때문에 피해야 한다.</li>
<li>긴 테스트 에러 발생 시 종료하지 않고 에러만 표시할 경우 soft assertion 사용 가능하다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Make a few checks that will not stop the test when failed...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> expect.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">soft</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTestId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'status'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toHaveText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Success'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ... and continue the test to check more things.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'link'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'next page'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
<li>
<p>third-party 종속된 테스트는 피하라</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'**/api/fetch_data_third_party_dependency'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  route.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fulfill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    status: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    body: testData,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goto</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'https://example.com'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="best-practices-1">Best Practices<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#best-practices-1"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p><strong>playwright에 내장된 locator를 사용해라</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>auto waiting, retry-ability 등의 기능을 사용하기 위해서 내장된 locator를 이용</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">👍 page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'submit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>Use chaining and filtering</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>페이지의 특정 부분을 검색할 경우 chaning, filtering으로 좁히는 걸 추천</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> product</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'listitem'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ hasText: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Product 2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> });</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'listitem'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ hasText: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Product 2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Add to cart'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>Prefer user-facing attributes to XPath or CSS selectors</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>유저 중심의 속성 셀렉터를 사용</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">👎 page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button.buttonIcon.episode-actions-later'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">👍 page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, { name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'submit'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> })</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>codegen을 활용한 locators 자동생성</strong></p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright codegen playwright.dev</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  👍 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> expect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByText</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'welcome'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toBeVisible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>

<h4 id="local-debugging">Local debugging<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#local-debugging"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<ul>
<li>vscode extension을 활용해서 디버깅하는 것을 추천
<img src="/blog-assets/2023/04/assets/02/Untitled_4.png" alt="Untitled"></li>
<li>command 방식으로 디버깅 가능</li>
<li>특정 테스트 파일, 테스트 라인 번호를 추가해서 디버깅 가능</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">--</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">debug</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npx playwright test example.spec.ts:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">9</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> --</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">debug</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="playwright-현실적인-적용방안">Playwright 현실적인 적용방안<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#playwright-현실적인-적용방안"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>마케팅 툴에 대한 예외처리: 진행 중
<ul>
<li>테스트 자동화 툴로 실행된 환경에서 GA, Braze 등의 마케팅 툴에 대해서 동작하지 않도록 수정(navigator.webdriver 이용한 분기)
<ul>
<li><a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Navigator/webdriver">Navigator: webdriver property - Web APIs | MDN</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>지원 브라우저/플랫폼에 대한 정의 CTO팀 스펙논의 필요
<ul>
<li>Playwright에서 지원하는 크로미움, 파포, 사파리, 모웹(AOS, IOS)에 대한 서비스만에 기준이 필요</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>E2E 테스트에 에러에 대한 대응 자세 CTO팀 스펙논의 필요
<ul>
<li>각 팀별로 에러 발생 시 어떻게 대응할지에 대한 전략이 필요(담당자 지정) → 슬랙으로 노티는 필수</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="e2e-테스트-자동화-구축을-위해서-어떤-걸-해야할까">E2E 테스트 자동화 구축을 위해서 어떤 걸 해야할까?<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#e2e-테스트-자동화-구축을-위해서-어떤-걸-해야할까"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h4 id="시나리오-작성기획qa테스트-엔지니어">시나리오 작성(기획/QA/테스트 엔지니어)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#시나리오-작성기획qa테스트-엔지니어"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<ul>
<li>사용자 인터페이스(UI)를 고려한 테스팅 시나리오 작성</li>
<li>수동으로 했던 테스트를 자동화 할 수 있는 테스트 시나리오 작성(라이브 배포 sanity 검증, 기본적인 검증)</li>
<li>Happy 케이스만이 아닌 Bad 케이스에 대한 시나리오 작성</li>
<li>개발 진행 시 테스트 코드 작성한다면 BAT 테스트 일정을 줄이는게 가능</li>
<li>기능/컴포넌트 단위 테스트를 자동화로 이관하고, 사용성 테스트를 중점으로 테스트 가능</li>
</ul>
<p>UX 우선 조직 - 시나리오 작성 편</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/*</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 로그인 동작 대한 시나리오 작성</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 1. 테스트의 동작을 기술한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 2. 테스트 수행할 행동에 대해서 기술한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 3. /login 방문한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 4. 이메일 입력 타이틀 엘리먼트 선택한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 5. input 엘리먼트에 fake@email.com 입력한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 6. 비밀번호 입력 타이틀 엘리먼트 선택한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 7. input 엘리먼트에 1234 입력한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 8. 로그인 버튼을 클릭 한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> * 사용자 인터페이스(UI)를 고려한 시나리오 작성이 필수....</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC"> */</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'account enroll'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">page</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">goto</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/login'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTitle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'이메일 입력'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(process.env.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">USER_ID</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByTitle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'비밀번호 입력'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(process.env.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">USER_PASSWORD</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> page.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">locator</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'.account'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getByRole</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'button'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">click</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4 id="테스트-코드-유지보수-진행여부기획qa팀과-논의필요">테스트 코드 유지보수 진행여부(기획/QA팀과 논의필요)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#테스트-코드-유지보수-진행여부기획qa팀과-논의필요"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h4>
<ul>
<li>QA팀에서도 진행하고 있는 E2E 테스트와 중첩되지 않도록 진행 가능여부</li>
<li>사용자 입장에서 꼭 필요한 테스트에 대한 리스트화 필요</li>
<li>서비스 테스트 자동화 확산
<ul>
<li>성격에 맞는 테스트 자동화 필요
<ul>
<li>간소화 테스트 개인 의견
<ul>
<li>테스트 속도 빨라야함(5분 내 → 사람이 테스트 한 것보다 빨라야함) - 핫픽스 테스트 시 5분 정도 소요</li>
<li>성공/실패를 하더라도 빌드에 영향은 없어야한다.</li>
<li>실패하더라도 노티는 하지만, 배포/개발 프로세스에 따로 block 되면 안된다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>그외 테스트
<ul>
<li>테스트 케이스가 많아 질 경우 도메인 , 기능단위 별로 젠킨스잡/테스트 구성을 다르게 가져간다.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="기대결과">기대결과<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#기대결과"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>자동화 테스트를 통해서 배포 후에 이슈(버그)를 빠르게 찾을 수 있어 <strong>유저의 경험을 개선</strong>시킬 수 있다.</li>
<li>기본적인 사용자 종단 테스트를 통해서 <strong>서비스의 안정화</strong>를 꾀할 수 있다.</li>
<li><strong>개발 코드의 신뢰도 상승과 코드의 품질</strong>을 높일 수 있다.</li>
<li>새로운 기능에 추가 시 기존 기능까지 같이 테스트를 함으로써 <strong>잠재적인 이슈에 대해서 테스팅이 가능</strong>합니다.</li>
<li>멀리 볼 경우 <strong>시간/비용을 절약</strong>할 수 있습니다.</li>
</ul>
